SELECT p."Id", p."BatchNumber", p."Product", p."Shift", p."ProductionLineId", p1."Name" AS "ProductionLineName", p1."Code" AS "ProductionLineCode", p."OperatorId", p."OperatorName", p."StartedAt", p."CompletedAt", p."TargetQuantity", p."ProducedQuantity", p."Status", p."CreatedAt"
FROM aquora_tenant_code_company."ProductionBatches" AS p
